**1. `SiegeDefenseBehavior`'s header comment describes the opposite of the code beneath it.** [Verified] `Main/Features/Siege/SiegeDefenseBehavior.cs:93` reads `the split bought nothing and was reverted`; the split is live four lines below. `OnHourlyTick` (`:97`) guards `_service.OnHourlyTickShared()` behind `if (_coopSession.IsAuthority)` (`:100-101`), then calls `_service.OnHourlyTickLocalPlayer()` unguarded (`:106`). Both halves exist on `ISiegeDefenseService.cs:26`/`:36` and in `SiegeDefenseService.cs:164`/`:181`. [Verified] `git show 46ce6436 -- Main/Features/Siege/SiegeDefenseBehavior.cs` puts comment and code in the **same commit** (`fix(coopinterop): presence is not authority`, Sat Aug 1 20:26:41 2026): one hunk deletes `if (!_coopSession.IsAuthority) return; _service.OnHourlyTick();`, adds the split, and adds the paragraph claiming the split was reverted. `git log -S OnHourlyTickShared` on service and interface returns that one commit, so the split has no earlier life in git. [Verified] The commit message tells a third story (`The shared/local split landed earlier so a client could still be rewarded for a siege it defended, but GrantReward set RewardClaimed ... Clients now record the claim in non-persisted process state, behind a named predicate`), describing the split as kept and refined, and its "landed earlier" does not match the git record. [Verified] The stale narrative is duplicated into `TAOM.Tests/Features/CoopInterop/CoopAuthorityGateTests.cs:128-129` (`a 2026-08-01 change split this and had to be reverted`). Four artifacts disagree: the code has the split, two comments say reverted, the commit message says retained, and only `docs/features/coop-interop.md:233-248` (paragraph opens `:233`, two-row Half/Method/Gate table `:238-241`, prose to `:248`) matches the code. **Correction to the earlier pass:** the citation `:239-247` was off; `:239` is a markdown separator row and the section's last sentence is on `:248`. **Owner: TAOM.**

**5. Four TAOM features mutate shared campaign state from a global tick with no co-op gate.** [Inference] for the whole group: these are review candidates, not confirmed bugs. Verified is the mechanical precondition (global tick registered, zero co-op provider references, reachable shared-state write); not established is that any of them diverges in a live session, and none was observed failing. [Verified] for each: a grep for `ICoopSessionProvider|ICoopPresenceProvider|IsAuthority|ShouldDeferToHost|IsCoopActive|IDedicatedServerProvider` across each feature directory returned **no hits**, so there is no gate anywhere in any of the four. Line-level "no gate at `:N`" pointers from the earlier pass are dropped: absence is a property of the directory, not of a line.

| Feature | Verdict |
|---|---|
| `SupplyLines` | Mutates the `SyncData` order book (`_taomSupplyOrders` / `_taomSupplyOrderCounter`, `Hooks/SupplyLinesCampaignBehavior.cs:68-69`) and creates world parties via `MobileParty.CreateParty` (`SupplyCaravanService.cs:163`) from `AdvanceOrders` (`SupplyOrderService.cs:338`), reached from both `HourlyTick` (`:205`, call at `:209`) and `FrameTick` (`:213`, call at `:226`). Both ride global ticks registered at `Hooks/SupplyLinesCampaignBehavior.cs:53-54`; handler `OnHourlyTick` at `:126` calls `_orders.HourlyTick()` at `:130`. **Correction to the brief:** the `Hero.MainHero` gold charge (`ChargePlayer`, defined `SupplyOrderService.cs:477`) has exactly one call site, `:185`, inside `TryPlaceOrder` (`:93`), a player-initiated path, not the tick. Tick exposure is party creation and the order book. |
| `Refuge` | Mutates every refuge party's disband queue: `RefugeService.HourlyTick` (`:382`) loops `_refuges.Keys` calling `CancelDisband(partyId)` at `:393` on every peer, driven by `HourlyTickEvent` registered at `Hooks/RefugeCampaignBehavior.cs:52`. Party creation (`MobileParty.CreateParty`, `RefugeService.cs:867`, inside `SpawnRefugeParty` at `:860`) and hero creation (`HeroCreator.CreateSpecialHero`, `WardenService.cs:217`) sit on the player-initiated founding path calling `SpawnRefugeParty` at `RefugeService.cs:184`, not the tick. |
| `FieldCamp` | **Correction to the earlier pass, the mechanism was wrong.** `CampService.HourlyTick` (`:289-331`) does **not** call `HoldMainParty`. `HoldMainParty` has three call sites: `:224` inside `Establish(CampType)` (`:206`, player-initiated), and `:530`/`:542` inside `MoveGuardTick` (`:521`), called only from `FrameTick` (`:333`) at `:351`; `FrameTick` is driven by `TickEvent`, registered at `Hooks/FieldCampCampaignBehavior.cs:53` and dispatched at `:135`. So the `MobileParty.MainParty?.SetMoveModeHold()` write (`CampService.cs:671`) rides the per-frame tick, not the hourly one. The hourly tick (registered `:52`, dispatched through `OnHourlyTick` `:120` calling `_camps.HourlyTick()` `:125`) carries its own ungated writes: `BreakPlayerCamp()` at `:301` and `:308`, `AddMoraleToMainParty` at `:324` (which does `MobileParty.MainParty.RecentEventsMorale += delta`, `:768`), and `ForageHour(camp)` at `:329` (`:482`). Two global ticks, both ungated. **The compat already found this feature and handled it externally** (finding 10). |
| `CultureMarketplace` | Mutates `Settlement.ItemRoster` via `_townAdapter.RemoveItem` (`CultureMarketplaceMaintenanceService.cs:80`), reached from `OnDailyTickSettlement` (`CultureMarketplaceBehavior.cs:143`) at `:159-160`. **Correction to the brief:** the mutation rides `DailyTickSettlementEvent` (registered `:70`), not `DailyTickEvent` (`:71`); the plain `OnDailyTick` at `:74` only flushes a log digest. `:82` notes `No SyncData`, so divergence would live in vanilla settlement rosters rather than a TAOM save key. Path note: this behaviour is `Main/Features/CultureMarketplace/CultureMarketplaceBehavior.cs`, with no `Hooks/` subdirectory, unlike the other three. |

**Owner: TAOM.**

### Compat-side, reported to Sternab, not actionable here

**6. `CoopPatchShieldComponent` converts a hard API-contract failure into a log line and boots anyway.** [Verified] `CoopPatchShieldPolicy.Install` throws `MissingMethodException("TAOM co-op presence/PatchShield API changed")` at `CoopPatchShieldPolicy.cs:39` when the reflected `CoopPresence.IsActive` getter or `PatchShield.Install` no longer matches (check at `:37`: `methodInfo?.ReturnType != typeof(bool) || methodInfo2?.ReturnType != typeof(void)`). [Verified] `CoopPatchShieldComponent.TryInstallEarly` wraps the call in `catch (Exception ex)` (`:49-52`), assigns `_status = "WARNING: early co-op policy unavailable: " + ex.GetType().Name + ": " + ex.Message` at `:51`, writes it with `Console.WriteLine` at `:53`, and returns normally. Nothing faults, nothing retries. [Verified] The throw at `:39` precedes the two `PatchOnce` calls at `:42-43` and the unpatch sweep at `:45-54`, so neither effect lands: `CoopPresence.IsActive` is not forced true and `PatchShield.Install` is not suppressed. TAOM's own `Dependencies/Foundation/PatchShield.cs:125-137` skips installing when `CoopPresence.IsActive`, logging `PatchShield install skipped (finalizer tax on Coop's AutoSync surface)` at `:135`, so a silent degradation here means the shield installs across Coop's patched surface. [Verified] The cost is documented at `PatchShield.cs:50-59`: a shield finalizer binds `__originalMethod`, so Harmony's wrapper pays a `MethodBase.GetMethodFromHandle` plus try/catch **on every call**, noted there as `~50µs`, which is what produced the 104-109s tournament exit freeze. `docs/reviews/rca-tournament-exit-hang-2026-07-06.md:33` backs the number (`ReleaseMovie=104,482ms` and `108,866ms`, gen0 `+8,276` in all three measured hangs) and records the fix at `:49` (exit 105-109s down to 9.5s). A rename on TAOM's side therefore surfaces as one console WARNING plus a per-call tax, not a refusal to load. **Correction to the earlier pass:** the co-op skip block is `:125-137` (return at `:137`) and the cost comment is `:50-59`. **Owner: compat.**

**7. Package hygiene: two host documents are byte-identical, and the host package declares the client's id.** [Verified] In `TAOM-Coop-v0.3.15-CompatOnly-HOST-OPERATOR-340a0657-8295bcb5-r2/`, `WHAT-CHANGED.txt` and `LIVE-TEST-SUMMARY.txt` are the same file: 2134 bytes each, `diff` exits clean, both md5 `baf13f1f2804dee35ff3709e03f57c4c`. The content is a changelog (`:1` is `TAOM COOP v0.3.15 - SERVER SETTINGS AND DIAGNOSTICS`, `:7` is `Changes since 0.3.14:`), so the file promising a live-test summary delivers a changelog instead. The settings protocol number disagrees between the machine-readable manifest and everything else.** [Verified] `PACKAGE.json`'s `protocols` block opens at `:17` and closes at `:23`; the stale value is `"taomServerSettings":  2` at `:22`. The prose and the assembly agree on 3; the single stale value is the machine-readable `protocols` block, and any tooling gating compatibility on the structured field rather than the prose reads the wrong number, both inside the same 17815-byte file. **Owner: compat.**

| `ExecuteOpenCampMenu` IL length | 54 | 54 | **passes** |

[Verified] The seams themselves are intact: `Main/Features/FieldCamp/UI/FieldCampOverlayVM.cs` still declares `Refresh()` at `:173` and `ExecuteOpenCampMenu()` at `:212`, writing `ShowCampButton` (`:79`) and `CanMakeCamp` (`:92`). So the MVID is strictly stricter than the contract it guards: it trips on a binary the IL-shape pins would have accepted. [Verified] The consequence is module-wide, not component-local: `ComponentRegistry.cs:94-108` catches the install failure, unpatches (`:97`), deactivates (`:98`), and for a critical component calls `CompatState.MarkFaulted("critical component \"...\" failed to install")` (`:101`); the loop then hits `if (CompatState.Faulted) RollBackAll("module faulted during install")` (`:105-107`). One MVID mismatch tears down every component the compat installed.

**Correction, twice over, on the mechanism.** An earlier pass said the MVID is regenerated on every compile; a later pass called that wrong because `Main/TAOM.csproj:5-6` sets `<Deterministic>true</Deterministic>` and `<PathMap>`. The first pass was right, for a reason neither had read. [Verified] `Directory.Build.props:23` defaults `TaomBuildStamp` to `$([System.DateTime]::UtcNow.ToString('yyyyMMdd-HHmmss'))Z` and `:24` feeds it into `InformationalVersion`, which is emitted as a real assembly attribute and is therefore a compilation input. Deterministic hashing over an input containing the current second yields a fresh MVID per build. Measured: four MVIDs off this repo in one day (`40f9ed03`, `7c21ec91`, `b5f89f5b`, `b6786d7a`), none matching the pin. So the pin cannot be reproduced by checking out `v2.0.26` and rebuilding; it can only be matched by the exact artifact Sternab pinned against, which TAOM no longer has and cannot regenerate. **Correction on "every release invalidates the pin by construction."** The module version lives in `Main/_Module/SubModule.xml:6` (currently `<Version value="v2.0.28" />`) and never reaches the assembly: `Main/TAOM.csproj:4` hardcodes `<Version>2.0.0</Version>`, there is no hand-written `AssemblyInfo.cs` under `Main/`, and `build.ps1` contains zero references to `SubModule`. That is why the assembly-identity half of the pin still passes at 2.0.28. But the MVID half is invalidated by any build at all, not merely by a source change. **Owner: shared.** TAOM cannot fix a hash of its own binary after the fact, and the compat cannot re-derive one; what TAOM *can* do, cheaply, is set `TaomBuildStamp` from the release tag so future releases are reproducible.

## Open questions (need runtime, not source)

**1. How does Coop discover the compat's `IHandler` types?** 16 handler classes, 10 under `Coop.Core.Client.Services.TaomCoopCompat` and 6 under `Coop.Core.Server.Services.TaomCoopCompat`, each `public sealed class ... : IHandler, IDisposable`; upstream already declares `public interface IHandler : IDisposable` (`source/Common/Messaging/IHandler.cs:5`), so the second interface is decompiler output, not a second contract [Verified].
- No registration and no compile-time coupling: `TAOM.CoopCompat.csproj` carries exactly 16 `<Reference>` entries (`Common`, `LiteNetLib`, `GameInterface`, `Missions`, `Serilog`, `protobuf-net.Core`, `0Harmony`, `System.Core`, `System.Xml`, `TAOM`, six `TaleWorlds.*`), and `autofac` / `ContainerBuilder` / `IContainer` / `RegisterType` / `Autofac.Module` return zero hits across both decompiled trees [Verified]. Coop types are reached by string: `AccessTools.TypeByName("Coop.Core.Server.CoopServer")` (`CampaignWorldCoalescerComponent.cs:105`), a `FullName` compare against `Coop.Core.Client.Services.Heroes.Messages.NetworkNewPlayerHeroCreated` (`CompressedHeroJoinTransferClientHandler.cs:50`); ctors take only `Common` types, e.g. `FullWarDiplomacyClientHandler(IMessageBroker broker, INetwork network)` [Verified].
- The namespace **is** the registration. `CommonModule.RegisterAllTypesWithInterface<TModule, TInterface>` (`source/Coop.Core/Common/CommonModule.cs:63`) calls `InterfaceCollector.GetInterfaces<TInterface>(typeof(TModule).Namespace)` (`source/Common/Util/InterfaceCollector.cs:10`), which keeps concrete types only (class, non-generic, non-abstract) and calls `AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetDomainTypes(prefix)` (`source/Common/Extensions/ReflectionExtensions.cs:20`): it walks `domain.GetAssemblies()`, skips null namespaces (:33), keeps every `type.Namespace.StartsWith(namespacePrefix)` (:34) [Verified]. `ClientModule` (namespace `Coop.Core.Client`, `ClientModule.cs:26`, call at :83) and `ServerModule` (`Coop.Core.Server`, `ServerModule.cs:35`, call at :131) both pass `autoInstantiate: true` (Autofac `AutoActivate()`, so ctors fire at container build) with an `IPacketHandler` pass on the next line; the compat implements zero `IPacketHandler` [Verified]. A plain string prefix over every loaded assembly matches a foreign DLL.
- The seam is deliberate: Coop's other plugin interface `ICoopCommand` registers via `builder.RegisterAssemblyTypes(typeof(GameInterfaceModule).Assembly)` (`source/GameInterface/GameInterfaceModule.cs:89`), scoped to `GameInterface.dll` and unreachable from outside, which is why the compat's five console commands are `[CommandLineArgumentFunction]` methods instead [Verified].
- Confirmed from upstream source, not against the targeted binary. Coop checkout read is `development` at `v0.1.3-470-g4ad976c8c` (commit `4ad976c8c`, 2026-09-06, newest tag `v0.1.3`); `PACKAGE.json` pins `"coop": "v0.1.4"` with `coopIdentity: workshop-3770450698-v0.1.4-20260830`, the same Workshop item `docs/research/bannerlordcoop-internals.md` analysed at v0.0.3 [Verified]. Payload is 4 DLLs, 2 distinct (`Bootstrap.dll` SHA-256 `6EB31BCB...`, `TAOM.CoopCompat.dll` `86ACFBC4...`); `Coop.Core.dll` is absent but pinned in `supportedBaseline.coopPins` at `45924C8D5794D39EBF2A2E5E6206394837F25DFBFBD739A84C7163D191EABFB2`, so "does released 0.1.4 still scan by prefix" is answerable offline [Verified].
- What needs runtime is ordering: `TAOM.CoopCompat.dll` in the AppDomain before `ContainerBuilder.Build()`. `SubModule.xml` declares `TAOM.CoopCompat.Bootstrap.BootstrapSubModule` first ("MUST remain first. Its constructor establishes exact Coop dependency bindings before Bannerlord scans the following assembly's TypeDefs") then `TAOM.CoopCompat.CoopCompatSubModule`, plus `<DependedModuleMetadata id="CoopNightly" order="LoadBeforeThis" version="v0.1.4" />`; `CoopCompatSubModule`'s ctor calls `CoopPatchShieldComponent.TryInstallEarly()` (`CoopCompatSubModule.cs:171`) [Verified]. [Inference] module init forces the load long before any connect. Silent failure mode: `GetDomainTypes` wraps each `GetTypes()` in `catch (ReflectionTypeLoadException) { }` (`ReflectionExtensions.cs:39`), so a type-load failure drops the whole assembly from the scan with no log [Verified].

**2. The large-battle hard hang: TAOM-side, Coop-side or compat-side?** `KNOWN-ISSUES.txt` lists it third of 11 runtime-unproven areas, worded **"large-battle hard hangs (new heartbeat diagnostics, not a claimed cure);"**; `LIVE-TEST-SUMMARY.txt` says the release "does not claim to fix" it; `liveAcceptance.battleHangRootCauseResolved = false`, and `knownOpen` 6 repeats it [Verified].
- 0.3.15 shipped instrumentation only. `BattleFrameHitchProbeComponent` (`Name => "battle-frame-hitch-probe"`) pulses a `MainThreadHeartbeat` over `CompatLog.WriteHeartbeat` from `OnApplicationTick` with `active = (Mission.Current != null || Campaign.Current != null)`; a `System.Threading.Timer` at 5000 ms due time and 5000 ms period calls `MainThreadStallPolicy.Observe`, which returns `Stalled` when the last pulse is at least **15.0** s old, re-warns no oftener than **60.0** s, returns `Recovered` once pulses resume, `None` when inactive [Verified]. Attribution between a TAOM battle-path behaviour tree or GameModel, Coop serialization or its message pump, and a compat component is unresolved by anything in the package.
- Not a Coop-side suspect: `knownOpen` 32 ("Coop v0.1.4 now owns the `BinaryPackageFactory` recursive serialization-graph lifetime; exact full-CLR smoke passes, while live repeated-battle and long-session behavior remains runtime-unproven") records an upstream ownership handoff, not an open defect the compat works around [Verified].

**3. Does Sternab have written permission to ship types inside Coop's namespaces?** Not determinable from the artifacts. The package is 4 `.txt`, 4 `.json`, 3 `.ps1`, 3 `.cmd`, 1 `.xml`, 1 `.sha256`, 4 `.dll`, no `.md`; a case-insensitive search for `permission`, `licen`, `copyright`, `maintainer`, `consent`, `Sternab` over every non-DLL file returns two hits, both the identical ACL message `Windows administrator permission is needed to write to this Steam folder.` (`Tools/Install-Client.ps1:667`, `Tools/Rollback-Client.ps1:226`), and all four DLLs return zero under ASCII and UTF-16LE string scans, so no embedded licence resource and no populated copyright attribute [Verified].
- Per question 1 the placement is load-bearing, not decorative. TAOM's note that BannerlordCoop is public with no no-decompile policy and "ships 893 of its own generated .cs files in plaintext" (`Dependencies/Foundation/CoopPresence.cs:75-77`) covers reading Coop's code, not publishing into its namespace [Verified]. Answer by asking the two authors; the Workshop listing, Nexus page and Discord were not read.

**4. The 11 areas the package flags as statically covered but runtime-unproven.** Verbatim from `KNOWN-ISSUES.txt`, in its numbering [Verified]: 1 flag-free PatchShield policy across actual module-load orders; 2 voted call-to-war offers and kingdom-menu freezes with multiple players; 3 large-battle hard hangs (new heartbeat diagnostics, not a claimed cure); 4 Steam lobby publication on hosts reporting discovery failures; 5 transferred-save join and reconnect reconciliation; 6 revised bridge/chokepoint navigation under several connected clients; 7 careers immediately after character possession and after reconnect; 8 large siege destructible-state transitions; 9 long-session serialization and campaign-world recovery; 10 TAOM settings consumption with non-default values; 11 authoritative troop and settlement-stock commands.
- One behavioural gap in the same file is not a test question: "The field-camp action is hidden in co-op for now because its TAOM behavior is not yet synchronized by the server" [Verified].

**5. What `liveAcceptance` means in practice.** 43 keys: one `status` (`"v0.3.15-coop-v0.1.4-migration-runtime-unproven"`) plus **42 boolean criteria, all 42 `false`** [Verified, counted from the parsed JSON]. Siblings agree: `diagnostics.runtimeProven` false, `modules["TAOM.CoopCompat"].runtimeQualified` false, `modules["TAOM.CoopCompat"].sourceDirty` true (`wrapper.sourceDirty` false), `knownOpen` at **33** prose entries [Verified]. Criteria that gate other sections: `patchShieldRuntimeProven`, `battleHangRootCauseResolved`, `careerAuthorityRuntimeProven`, `authoritativePositionV2RuntimeProven`, `taomSettingConsumptionProofRuntimeProven`, `joinBaselineReconciliationRuntimeProven`, `serializationGraphLifetimeRuntimeProven`, `deterministicBattleSceneRuntimeProven` [Verified].
- The question is which single session flips the most, and which criteria have a checkable observable. `liveAcceptance` names no procedure for any of the 42; 16 of the 33 `knownOpen` entries carry an imperative (test, confirm, verify, retest, reproduce, report, collect), entry 16 being the model: "FullWar client reconciliation is statically qualified but runtime-unproven; confirm the client log contains a Gondor-Mordor probe after joining" [Verified]. 33 entries cannot map one-to-one onto 42 criteria, so some criteria have no acceptance step anywhere in the package.

**6. Do TAOM and the compat truly share one Harmony registry?** Already open as question 1 in `docs/research/bannerlordcoop-internals.md:331` against Coop v0.0.3; new here are v0.1.4 and the module-id finding below [Verified]. The compat's design bets yes: `CoopPatchShieldPolicy.Install` calls `Harmony.GetAllPatchedMethods()`, filters on `Harmony.GetPatchInfo(m).Owners.Contains("TAOM.Dependencies.Foundation.PatchShield")`, and `Unpatch`es every hit with `HarmonyPatchType` `0` (All), returning the count (`CoopPatchShieldPolicy.cs:45-55`) [Verified]. HarmonyLib's registry is per-assembly-instance static state, so that loop returns nothing unless both mods resolve the same `0Harmony`. TAOM's `[HarmonyCensus]` was written to answer this: "If its owner id never appears here while the game plainly runs its patches, there are two Harmony instances and TAOM's view of the process is blind" (`Main/Features/CoopInterop/Diagnostics/HarmonyCensusReportBuilder.cs`) [Verified].
- A gate blocks it. The census has one call site, `Main/SubModule.cs:1643`, under `if (coopIds.Count > 0)` at :1641 over `CoopPresence.ActiveCoopModuleIds`. `CoopPresence.CompiledModuleDefaults` and the shipped `Dependencies/_Module/coop-modules.txt` are both `{ "BannerlordTogether", "BattleLinkMPClient", "Coop" }` with an empty `[harmony-owner-prefixes]` section; `CoopNightly` appears nowhere under `Dependencies/` or `Main/`, while `launcherOrder` is `["CoopNightly", "TAOM.CoopCompat"]` and the compat's `SubModule.xml` declares `<DependedModule Id="CoopNightly" />` among five depended modules with no `Coop` entry [Verified]. `CoopPresencePolicy.MatchKnown` is set membership under `StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase`, not a prefix test, so `CoopNightly` never matches `Coop`: on the shipped tuple `ActiveCoopModuleIds` resolves empty and **the census never prints** unless the player adds `CoopNightly` to `coop-modules.txt` or drops `coop-force-active.flag` into the `TAOM.Dependencies` module directory, which makes `CoopPresencePolicy.ResolveActiveIds` return the marker `"(forced-by-flag)"` (only when detection found nothing) and satisfies the count gate [Verified].
- Second casualty of the same gate: `SettingsFingerprintLog.WriteAcross` sits inside that `if` block (`Main/SubModule.cs:1659-1664`), so no peer prints a settings fingerprint either, which is the observable `taomSettingConsumptionProofRuntimeProven` would lean on [Verified]. The compat author knows both hatches (`supportedBaseline.taomCoopPolicyFlags` names `coop-force-active.flag` and `patchshield-disabled.flag`; `taomCoopModuleIds` is `["Coop", "CoopNightly"]`), but its own policy does not open the gate: two prefixes at Harmony priority 800 under owner `taom.coopcompat.coop-patchshield-policy`, on `CoopPresence.IsActive`'s getter (`ReportCoopActive`, forces `__result = true`, skips the original) and on `PatchShield.Install` (`SkipShieldInstall`, returns false), neither touching `ActiveCoopModuleIds`, a separate unpatched property over the same backing list (`CoopPresence.cs:92-101`) [Verified]. [Inference] no `[HarmonyCensus]` block has yet been produced from this tuple because the default `CoopNightly` order suppresses it; cheapest fix in the next playtest.

**Where the evidence lands.** Compat lines go to `coopcompat-<pid>.log` in the first directory that resolves: `<BANNERLORD_USER_DIR>\logs\TAOM.CoopCompat\` when that variable holds a rooted path (checked first on every role, not only servers), else `Documents\Mount and Blade II Bannerlord\Logs\TAOM.CoopCompat\`, else `<LocalAppData>\TAOM.CoopCompat\Logs\` (`CompatLog.ResolveLogDirectory`); it rotates at 5 MiB keeping 12 files. Heartbeat lines are the exception: sidecar `coopcompat-<pid>.log.heartbeat`, which `CompatLog.WriteHeartbeat` silently stops appending to once it reaches 1,048,576 bytes, with no rotation. TAOM writes `Logs\taom_debug_<yyyy-MM-dd_HH-mm-ss>.log` relative to the game working directory, 10 retained (`Main/Core/Logging/FileLogger.cs`) [Verified].

| Q | Drive it, then read this |
|---|---|
| 1 DI | Join as a client, then check the host. `coopcompat-<pid>.log` must carry the five construction lines that fire only if Autofac activated the type (exhaustive; the other 11 handlers log nothing at construction): `authoritative-party-position \| client checkpoint handler constructed`, `career-authority \| client handler constructed; waiting for campaign possession`, `wotr-full-war-client-sync \| client handler constructed; awaiting CampaignReady`, `wotr-relay-client \| handler constructed; live momentum applies enabled`, server-side `career-authority \| server handler constructed; authenticated v1 endpoints ready` [Verified]. Absent on a working connection means the prefix scan never reached the assembly, or `GetTypes()` threw and it was swallowed at `ReflectionExtensions.cs:39` |
| 2 hang | Reproduce a large battle with the collectors on. Read the **sidecar**, not the main log: `main-thread-heartbeat \| <context> event=Stalled lastTickAgeSeconds=<n>; stale tick is diagnostic evidence, not proof of a deadlock`, then whether a matching `event=Recovered` follows. `<context>` is one of exactly four strings, `role={CLIENT\|SERVER} context={campaign\|mission}`; first warn 15 s, repeats no faster than 60 s, 5 s timer. The main log carries the window summary `battle-frame-hitch-probe \| <CLIENT\|SERVER> phase=<p> seconds=<s> frames=<n> avgFps=<f> maxMs=<m> over50=<n> over100=<n> over250=<n> over1000=<n>`; pair with the host's `Coop_server.log` (`diagnostics.coopDetailedFile`) [Verified] |
| 5 criteria | For `fullWarDiplomacyClientReconciliationRuntimeProven`: `wotr-full-war-client-sync \| Gondor-Mordor probe: serverAtWar=..., localBefore=..., localAfter=..., action=...`, which fires only for the `empire_s`/`empire_w` pair in either order under `StringComparison.Ordinal`, `action` one of `server-missing`, `already-at-war`, `observe-would-repair`, `repaired`, `failed-postcondition`, `exception`. For `itemStockCommandRuntimeProven` and the troop half, the compat registers `taom.item.give`, `taom.item.stock`, `taom.item.find`, `taom.troop.give`, `taom.troop.find` as `[CommandLineArgumentFunction]` methods; `knownOpen` gives the shapes `taom.item.stock <settlementId> <itemId> [modifierId] [count]` and `taom.troop.give <heroId> <troopId> <count>`, ids via `taom.troop.find <substring>` [Verified] |
| 6 Harmony | Add `CoopNightly` to `Dependencies/_Module/coop-modules.txt`, or drop `coop-force-active.flag` in the `TAOM.Dependencies` module directory, then start the game. Read `[HarmonyCensus] totalPatchedMethods=<n>, owners=<n>, coopActive=true, coopIds=[...]` plus one `[HarmonyCensus]   owner=<id> patches=<n>  targets: ...` row per owner; the direct answer is `[HarmonyCensus]   0Harmony instances (<n>): <path> v<version> \| ...`, which on `n > 1` is followed by a warning that the registry is per-instance and the owner list may be incomplete. Proof is any of `Bannerlord.Coop`, `Coop.UILoading`, `Coop.BootFix`, `CoopAutoRegistryFactory` (`PatchShieldPolicy.CompiledProtectedOwnerPrefixes`, [Verified] but read from v0.0.3 on 2026-08-01, so possibly drifted) beside a `taom.coopcompat.*` owner in one census. `contested` rows (capped at 60) and uncapped `TRANSPILER CONFLICT` rows come free [Verified] |
| 6 cheap | Needs no flag: `coop-patchshield-policy \| selected=<id>; co-op presence enforced; PatchShield install suppressed; existing shielded methods removed=<N>; no flag files required` in the main log, mirrored to stdout as `[TAOM.CoopCompat] <status>`. `N > 0` means the compat's Harmony saw TAOM PatchShield's owner, the shared-registry proof from the other side; `N = 0` is ambiguous (PatchShield may never have installed); `selected=` absent with `no selected Coop/CoopNightly module; no changes` means the probe never reached the registry walk [Verified] |

Fallback for pinning a desync to a specific object graph: Coop's five auditors, all `ICoopCommand` in the checkout read, `coop.debug.players.list` (`PlayerListCoopCommand`), `coop.debug.hero.audit` (`HeroAuditCoopCommand`), `coop.debug.mobileparty.audit` (`AuditPartiesCoopCommand`, whose `Prefix` is lowercase though the source comment above it writes `mobileParty`), `coop.debug.settlements.audit` (`AuditCoopCommand`), `coop.debug.town.auditor` (`AuditorCoopCommand`) [Verified]. They live in `GameInterface`, one of the five DLLs `supportedBaseline.coopPins` pins by SHA-256 [Verified]; survival into released v0.1.4 is [Inference], though `knownOpen` 20 instructs testers to use `coop.debug.players.list` [Verified].
